# DONE — the map, since 2026-09-12

Everything this project finished, decided or ratified until 2026-10-04. **Since
that day every entry is an issue file in `issues/`**, in the folder of the day
its name carries, its name the one it had here, and a closed item stays where
it was filed: nothing is moved into a record any more (records.md § The issues).

**This file is the map, and it keeps the 4071 lines the record had.** Each line
names the file holding what that line used to hold, so a `DONE.md:NNN` citation
still reaches its entry — there are 28 of them, 26 inside records §14 forbids
anybody to repoint. One, `DONE.md:924`, had been aimed at a blank line since it
was written and reached nothing; it names its entry now. On 2026-10-04 every row
was repointed into `issues/`, and not one was added or removed.

**Nothing here is open and nothing needs reading front to back.** It is a grep
target, and it is now two of them:

```
grep -rln "panel 062" issues/            # what a sitting decided
grep -n  "panel 062" docs/work/DONE.md  # which line of the old record held it
```

**The record is append-only and is never rewritten** (CLAUDE.md §14). An item
decided wrongly gets a *new* item saying so; the old one keeps its words,
because a record that edits its own past cannot be used to check anything.
`records/appended` performs that rule; `records/rotated` keeps this map's height.

**How an item gets here.** It does not, since 2026-10-04: an item is an issue
file from the day it is filed, and closing it ticks it where it stands. This map
answers the citations of the old record's lines, and nothing else.

The twenty-three entries nearest the top predate the checkbox convention and are
dated prose bullets from 2026-08-04, kept as written.
*******************************************************************************
**THE RECORD BEGINS HERE — oldest first, newest appended at the end**
